{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{write__corr__fun_8f90_a239e5f5f55d2e60197ce452cd5f21e10} 
subroutine bootstrap (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{integer}]{unit}{, }\item[{real(8), dimension(4)}]{av}{, }\item[{real(8), dimension(4)}]{err}{, }\item[{integer}]{nbin}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Performs bootstrap analysis for statistical error estimation. 

This subroutine performs bootstrap analysis to estimate statistical errors in correlation sampling data. It reads data from a file, performs multiple bootstrap resampling iterations, and computes averages and error bars for reference energy, reweighted energy, energy differences, and overlap calculations. 
\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em unit} & File unit number for reading data \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em av} & Array of averages (4 elements) \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em err} & Array of error bars (4 elements) \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{out}}  & {\em nbin} & Number of bins read from file \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

Writes correlation functions and related quantities to output files. 

This subroutine writes various correlation functions and related quantities to output files. It handles density, spin, structure factors, pair correlations, and other observables. The subroutine averages data over processors and computes error bars.

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{write\_rho()}{write\_rho()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{write__corr__fun_8f90_a891ffbce9acddafdabf1b0695b87edcc} 
subroutine write\+\_\+corr\+\_\+fun\+::write\+\_\+rho}



Writes electron density data to output files. 

This subroutine writes electron density data to output files in various formats including regular grid, pair correlation, and radial distribution functions. It handles both periodic and non-\/periodic systems with support for tilted cells. 

Definition at line \mbox{\hyperlink{write__corr__fun_8f90_source_l00259}{259}} of file \mbox{\hyperlink{write__corr__fun_8f90_source}{write\+\_\+corr\+\_\+fun.\+f90}}.
